## Description

<!-- SECTION:DESCRIPTION:BEGIN -->
Users should handle PR organization from the same view: title/body/base, ownership, reviewers, labels, milestone, linked issues, and project placement.

## Testing steps

1. Code/provider tests: Check picker pagination, update payloads, reviewer/team restrictions, concurrent edits, and partial multi-field failures.
2. Real-binary TUI E2E scenario `pr_metadata`: Edit metadata using keys and clicks, cancel once and submit once, then assert unchanged fields, exact commands, refreshed detail, and disabled unsupported controls. Run fresh fixtures for keyboard and mouse paths and verify equivalent outcomes at 120x40 and 80x24 where applicable.
3. Run `cargo test --test tui_e2e pr_metadata -- --nocapture` and verify a nonzero selected test count; run the relevant code/provider tests and `cargo test --all-targets`. Record actual results and failure artifacts before completion. The initial framework is delivered by the bootstrap/framework prerequisites.
<!-- SECTION:DESCRIPTION:END -->

## Acceptance Criteria
<!-- AC:BEGIN -->
- [x] #1 Metadata pickers load eligible values and preserve unmodified fields.
- [ ] #2 Reviewer/team, label, assignee, base, milestone, linked-issue and project operations map to verified host capabilities.
- [x] #3 Per-field permission/failure results are visible and server read-back confirms the change.
- [x] #4 Automated code/provider tests pass for the task-specific scenarios in Testing steps.
- [x] #5 Real-binary PTY E2E tests pass for the specified keyboard and mouse journeys; all exposed actions are clickable and keyboard accessible with equivalent effects.
- [x] #6 All displayed actors use the shared username/avatar or honest fallback; Git and CI states use the shared semantic colors with redundant labels.
<!-- AC:END -->

## Definition of Done
<!-- DOD:BEGIN -->
- [ ] #1 Run the task-specific code/provider tests and PTY scenario with nonzero selected tests; save command results and artifacts in task notes.
- [ ] #2 Run cargo test --all-targets, cargo fmt --check, and cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings; fix failures before marking Done.
- [ ] #3 Verify mouse/keyboard parity and applicable resize/loading/error states using the shared framework; no placeholder or ignored required E2E tests.
<!-- DOD:END -->
